# Skills · D · part 44

844 entries, debate to debt-payoff. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **debate**: Structured adversarial debate for hard decisions. Use when making any technical decision between 2+ options, architecture choices, library selection, REST vs GraphQL, monolith vs microservices, SQL vs NoSQL, or when the user says "should I", "which is better", "compare", "pros and cons", "trade-offs", "help me decide", "A or B", "what approach", or any variation of "I can't decide between". Also use when the user presents two options and seems uncertain which to pick, even if they don't explicitly ask for a debate. ([haytamAroui/AZ-CLAUDE](https://github.com/haytamAroui/AZ-CLAUDE/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/debate) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **debate**: 用于中文辩论备赛、辩题研究、证据搜集与核验、价值辩或政策辩框架、对方论证强化还原、反驳设计、质询、申论稿、结辩稿和材料审计。只要用户提到辩题、正反方、备赛、立论、攻防、证据卡、质询、辩稿或核验辩论资料，就应使用本技能；也适用于只做资料研究或审查现有材料。支持宿主工具、独立模型 API、单路或联合搜索、Crossref/OpenAlex/World Bank，以及基于结构化记录的规则门禁和成稿审计。 ([hellobiyue/debate-prep-workbench](https://github.com/hellobiyue/debate-prep-workbench/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)

- **debate**: Adversarial dialectic between two crew personas to stress-test a plan, design, claim, or decision before you commit to it. Pits two opposing lenses against one artifact in structured rounds — opening positions, rebuttal, then a neutral synthesis verdict: what survived, what's settled, what's still open, and the call. Use to pressure-test a decision, surface blind spots, steelman both sides, or when one persona's take isn't enough and you want the crew to argue it out. Name the two personas, or let it auto-pick the natural opposing pair. Trigger on: /debate, debate this, debate mode, argue bot… ([kridaydave/My_Skills](https://github.com/kridaydave/My_Skills/tree/HEAD/debate) · ★1)
- **debate**: Run a structured 3-agent debate on a candidate options entry, used when the primary signal lands in the 60-80 conviction boundary band — the band where calibration is typically weakest (high-conviction buckets often look great on a few wins but don't survive larger samples). Adversarial reasoning here surfaces edge cases the primary signal missed. ([mattohan567/tradingagent-template](https://github.com/mattohan567/tradingagent-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/debate) · ★1)

- **debate**: Multi-Agent Debate (Solver/Proposer/Critic/Defender/Checker 5エージェント反証) で主張・実装・判断を独立検証。学習源の異なるAI (Claude/Gemini/Codex/Grok+WebSearch) を意図的に対立させてハルシネーション・事実誤認を検出。v1.2 で forecast モード追加: 将来予測系の主張には MiroFish 式の agent-based simulation (ステークホルダー・ペルソナ世界を多ラウンド) でシナリオ分布を生成。v1.3 で precision 強化: Defender(擁護者)を追加し攻撃3:擁護0の非対称を解消、かつ「反論=有罪」をやめ Checker が一次情報で裏取りできた反論のみを採用 (false positive 抑制)。`/debate &lt;主張ファイル or テキスト&gt;` で起動。MARCH/Microsoft CORE研究に基づく実装で false positive -25.8% 期待。 ([AI-Driven-School/aiki](https://github.com/AI-Driven-School/aiki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/debate))

- **debate-9**: 收到 $立论 + 辩题且未注明持方时，启动九人赛制辩论：正反各四名辩手 + 一名评委 + 双方各一名资料员（共 11 个 teammate，Lead 当主席），按标准四对四赛制打 2/3/4 场，每场跑完 13 个发言单元，评委出 7 张技术票 + 1 张印象票并逐条论点提修改要求，场间双方各自修正论点；质询与自由辩论允许短攻防、照对方回答临时改口径，字数只卡每方实际发言总量；立论类稿按 260 字/分钟折成字数区间，辩手发言前用 count_speech.py 自查（答话、质询段不计入），出区间不许上场。最后交付正方、反方两套立论报告（md）+ 资料表（xlsx）+ 辩手稿（立论稿 / 申论稿 / 质询稿 / 小结稿 / 结辩稿 / 自由辩要点 / 数据资料）+ 一份共享的《辩论实录》。 ([Liz-van/debate-coach-skills](https://github.com/Liz-van/debate-coach-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/debate-9))

- **debate-consensus**: 合议制多智能体探索框架（"群议"）。用多个差异化人设的 agent 进群聊，通过辩论、投票、集体决议 探索开放性/模糊问题的稳健方案；内置 patch 安全三档（allow/needs-confirmation/blocked）、 VALIDATION 验证门控（先验证通过再 final answer）、后端状态机护栏（轮次/工具预算/检查点）。 与 san-sheng-liu-bu（科层编排）互补：三省六部管"复杂工程的任务编排把关"，群议管 "开放性问题的方案探索共识"。触发词：群议、合议、辩论投票、多智能体辩论、debate、consensus、 debate-consensus、集体决议、让多个模型讨论、合议共识、brainstorm with agents。 不做：不替代 san-sheng-liu-bu 的任务编排；不实现独立桌面应用（纯方法论 skill）。 ([clonetimer/Clonetimer-Skills](https://github.com/clonetimer/Clonetimer-Skills/tree/HEAD/debate-consensus))

- **debate-critic**: 当圆桌上已经出现表面一致、或所有人都同意某个方案时使用——专门寻找共识底下的空洞：共享的未验证前提、被忽略的反例、被偷换的概念。用于共识形成后的最后一道检查。 ([ovo-pwp/acp-room](https://github.com/ovo-pwp/acp-room/tree/HEAD/skills/debate-critic))
- **debate-decisions**: A compact decision protocol for non-trivial choices, reviews, governance calls, and live disagreement.
